# F14 readout — multi-factor long/short equity (2026-10-25)

Research only. Specification frozen in `docs/fund-program-preregistration-2026-10-25.md` (commit 43320cd). Composite A = sector-relative mean percentile of 12-1 momentum, quality (Piotroski; ROA for half-year filers), value (earnings yield + book-to-price), low 252-day vol; composite B adds 1-month reversal. Long = top quintile per sector, short = bottom quintile per sector (sectors with < 5 eligible names skipped); short leg hedged with a long index future at its trailing 252-day beta. Costs: market round trip at entry (UK stamp on buys and buy-to-cover), borrow 1%/yr (3% below 5 local units), micro round trip + roll on the hedge. Executable k = 5 and 10 per side, ≤ 2 names per sector, $30k tickets.

Pass rules: long leg — excess t ≥ 3 vs index and alpha t ≥ 2 (US: SPY+QQQ, beats QQQ); short leg — hedged-spread t ≥ 3; combined — t ≥ 3 vs zero; every window with trades profitable with PF ≥ 1.2 on spells; positive full sample. INCUBATION_ELIGIBLE: positive net excess, ≥ 3 of 4 windows positive, t ≥ 1.5, max DD < 50%.

Trials: 144. VALIDATION_PASS: 0. INCUBATION_ELIGIBLE: 14. 

## Construction caveat — the combined L/S book is net long the index

As pre-registered, combined L/S = long leg + hedged short leg, where the hedged short leg = −(short basket) + β × index future. The long leg carries ≈ +1 β, the short basket −1 β and the hedge +β (β of the short basket, 1.0–1.3 here), so the combined book has a **net index beta of roughly β, not zero**. Its t vs zero therefore mostly measures the index return over the sample, not spread alpha. The pre-registered verdict rule is kept, and the "Index diagnostics" columns (β to the index and alpha t after removing it; US: SPY+QQQ) show what remains. Any INCUBATION_ELIGIBLE L/S row whose alpha t is below 2 is beta, not a strategy. A market-neutral construction (long leg β-hedged too, or combined = long − short without the hedge) was not pre-registered and is not run here.

## Notes

- Trial count: the pre-registration counted 80 trials (k-books as one trial each); this run scores the k = 5 and k = 10 books as long, short and L/S separately, giving 144 trials. The deflated Sharpe is therefore computed over more trials than pre-registered (stricter); no book was added or removed.
- The "t" column is the monthly excess t vs the index for LONG books and the t vs zero for SHORT (hedged) and L/S books.
- Short-leg spells are scored net of borrow and costs; the hedge return is added at the portfolio level, not per spell.
- Delisted names exit at the last available close (a short that is delisted at zero is not modelled; shorts of acquired names exit at the last print).
- Ever-member names without a cached price or statement file are excluded (coverage table); the UK and continental samples are thin, so sector quintiles are often one or two names.
